# Welfare drafts, 2026-10-03: every organisation's first message

Review copy only: nothing is sent. One first message per organisation.

- **Funders (104).** A request to sponsor students: any size, including textbooks, transport or meals. The reason is a relationship the run verified from the funder's own pages (it funds a named home or programme). A funder with no verified relationship is held, because there is no honest reason to ask it yet. 29 are ready.
- **Children's homes and orphanages (99),** where children live in the home's care. 15 are ready.
- **Family-based and sponsorship programmes (159),** which support children who live with families, often paying school fees. 25 are ready.
- **Specialised centres (70):** disability and rehabilitation centres, street-children centres and safe houses. 5 are ready.
- Homes, programmes and centres all get the same first message: a partnership on the education of the children in their care, and a short meeting. No offer terms; the partner rate follows in the second message.
- **Introducer networks (7)**: bodies that could share the partnership with many homes. 5 are ready.
- A draft is held when the organisation has no published route, its only route is outside Tanzania, its email domain is outside Tanzania, the record carries red flags, the inbox is on a personal domain, the centre is specialised (confirm Silverleaf can meet the children's needs first) or the location is unresolved.
- Parent-enquiry replies are separate and held for the data-protection owner; they are not in this file.

Follow-up messages and the offer terms are in the run workbook. Companies are in the Employer Drafts file.

---

# 1. Funders: sponsorship request

## 1.1 Ready for review (29)

### 1.1.1 Africa Amini Alama: foreign NGO operating a children's home, English-medium schools and community programmes (medium priority)

- To: Mag. Dr. Cornelia Wallner-Frisee (President) | Route: office@africaaminialama.com (shared_email) | Plan: M1582f7c9bf6d | Status: draft_ready
- Reason for the ask: KinderVilla Momella (source https://africaaminialama.com/projekte/soziales, read 2026-10-02).

Subject: Sponsorship request: students at Silverleaf Academy

```
Dear Mag. Dr. Cornelia Wallner-Frisee,

My name is Mariam Haji, Marketing and Partnership Coordinator at Silverleaf Academy in Arusha. I understand that you are the President of Africa Amini Alama, which runs KinderVilla Momella. It is this commitment to supporting communities in Tanzania that led me to reach out to you directly.

We are seeking sponsorships for students to help cover school fees and other education-related costs. Support of any size helps, including a contribution towards textbooks, transport or meals. I would welcome the chance to share more about our school and students, and to discuss whether this might fit within Africa Amini Alama's current giving.

Would it be possible to arrange a short meeting, either in person or by phone, to discuss this further?

Thank you very much for your time and consideration.

Warm regards,
Mariam Haji
Marketing and Partnership Coordinator
Silverleaf Academy
```
